## Document 299: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

## Document 356: Monthly household spending indicator_ Spending resilient despite headwinds (2026-11-14)

Spending resilient despite headwinds
4 November 2026
Key points
• Household spending rose 0.6%mth in
September, returning to growth after
August’s brief decline. Q3 spending increased
2.1%qtr, while volumes rose a more subdued

0.7%qtr, pointing to a sharp acceleration
in price growth through the quarter.
• Services spending rebounded and goods
spending remained supportive. Non-
discretionary spending outperformed
discretionary categories, with higher fuel
spending providing significant support.

quarter was led by transport but with
most other categories also recording

in Q3, led by WA and Victoria.

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.
Spending returns to growth in September

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.

## Document 371: Rates Spark_ Higher rates amid a fragile calm

Source PDF: all report pdf/Rates Spark_ Higher rates amid a fragile calm.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Rates are back under upward pressure, reviving our tactical
bearish view. AI risks have become more idiosyncratic, but
upcoming catalysts could quickly reverse the move. France
remains under pressure, while Italy could see a rating upgrade

Michiel Tukker and Benjamin Schroeder
Tactically bearish on rates but AI event risk
ahead of us
Price action suggests global rates are happy to push higher again,
and we therefore return to our tactical bearish call. Risk sentiment is
improving, with AI worries turning more idiosyncratic, focused
mostly on NVIDIA’s leadership questions. Amid more stable risk
conditions, but without strong catalysts, the 2yr USD swap jumped
up by some 12bp on the day. Of course, one could argue oil ticked
higher and jobless numbers were robust, but nothing that would
warrant such a strong reaction by itself.
Buying the dip seems to be the recurring theme in equity markets,
but we warn of some key event risks ahead. Next week, we have

### Page 2

both Anthropic’s IPO pricing and NVIDIA announcing third-quarter
results. These two events are seen as important bellwethers for the
broader future of AI and can also shake the US macro outlook. From
the past weeks, we’ve seen how any disappointments can quickly
push US rates lower.
Europe is relatively more shielded from AI, but global risk sentiment
can be quick to pull down longer rates in particular. We argue that
the front end of the EUR and GBP curves should remain focused on

inﬂation, but the belly of the curve is more exposed to sentiment.
The euro swap curve still paints a very optimistic view of the ECB’s
terminal rate above 3%, which in our eyes can only hold if the
growth outlook improves materially. A global setback in AI would
push against this narrative, raising expectations for rate cuts after
the initial hiking cycle.
EGBs drift with the broader sentiment while
Italy is up for review by S&P
The mood in the European bond market has again darkened

somewhat. In part, this owed to the spillover from tech-related
equity jitters and unease around geopolitics after the latest
comments from US President Donald Trump, but the general
repricing higher of rates, which originated in the US, has done its
share as well.
France has lagged its peers with the 10y OAT/Bund back towards
150bp. There has been little news on the situation in France, but it is
worth noting that any hopes of ECB President Christine Lagarde

pulling France out of its political deadlock were dampened after the
ﬁrst polls this week showed she would get only 14% of the ﬁrst-
round votes, similar to other centrist candidates. This would still see
election frontrunner Marine Le Pen and left-wing rival Jean-Luc
Mélenchon advance to the second round, leaving one of the tail risk
scenarios on the table.
Italy, meanwhile, will face a scheduled review date by S&P, which
currently rates Italy BBB+ with a positive outlook. That outlook has

been in place since January, which would make a ﬁrst rating
upgrade back to single-A a possibility. Considering the more

## Document 375: Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the
Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX
Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW
12 November 2026 | 3:15 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Post the historical open on 11/6/2026, we saw a jaw-dropping 14% climb from day-
low last Friday. Finally took a breather (and a big one too) today; admittedly, I was
writing a different narrative, but last hour fade in Taiwan has forced me to rewrite this

(>1k points drop and picked up market turnover). So, where do we go from here?
Check out below for my observation and investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Takeaway
Sentiment. Sentiment is still cautious among foreigners; a lot of HFs faced serious
short squeeze from this rally. Yet, many are still reluctant to cover all their short
positions given the new short sell restrictions. and hence held on to the pain trades.
Interestingly, market still exhaust short sell quota almost every day. Additionally,

there is little conviction for a full-on rally and also low willingness to chase
around here given year-end. Overall, HFs have de-grossed much, one of the most
aggressive de-gross per GS prime book data. Key focus will be whether LOs are
willing to participate and to what extent will they add back the positions. Only
then, we will likely see a more sustainable rally further from here. Bottom line: are
we out of the woods? Not yet. Cautious optimism is perhaps the most positive

thing I can say for now.
Positioning. Foreigners have net sold for 10 straight sessions, totaling around
US$25.4bn. Most outflows are in TSMC but worth noting that we finally saw TSMC
net buy for the first time since 2 weeks yesterday. On the other hand, local funds
have been good size net buyers to absorb the foreign outflows, mainly from retail's
mutual fund subscription. Just regurgitating some of my berg blurbs from the past
few days, we still see lots of short interest in crowded long sectors, most in

ABF/CCL (i.e. NY Plastics) followed by CPO names; and most smid cap
outperformance was driven by short covering. The overly sold names saw little
attention and minimum desire to bottom fish (i.e. Delta, Chroma, KYEC). On a
brighter note, we also picked up some real money inflows into TSMC this week, but
I don't know whether it's enough to turn the tide. The more convicted long right
now for foreigners is still ASE in my opinion.
Retail. This is perhaps the most important factor now in Taiwan. Coupled with the

sentiment boost from National Stabilization Fund, many retail decided to hold
instead of cutting loss and even bought into the dip. For those retail that were
facing margin call during this correction, some were eventually saved by the market
rally. Cleaner positioning for sure, though perhaps not clean enough to fuel another
11/12/26, 3:20 PM Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/dc88e4dd-47de-45c1-bbf3-31c2c5c57299 1/11

### Page 2

10% climb in index. Per my local investor feedback, the smart pundits actually
many have missed out on this rally (of course they've also de-grossed much ahead).
Margin loan balance and maintenance ratio are two key data that I will follow
closely in the coming weeks.
Fundamentals. Fundamentals are still good with most company reporting record
high quarterly earnings. Monthly revenue also grew MoM. But stock trading is all
about forward looking. People are anticipating AI capex growth to slow down or

even plateau in the coming 2 years. Quanta (2382 TT) earnings call yesterday
validated this narrative - still high growth in 2027 but unlikely to see another triple-
digits rapid growth like 2026. Earnings outlooks probably matter less than
positioning and macro backdrop. With that, we will conclude most of our 3Q26
earnings this week with Hon Hai (2317 TT) as the main finale.
Liquidity Event: MSCI just published their quarterly review, and NYPCB (8046 TT)

and TUC (6274 TT) will be added into the index, while Lotes (3533 TT), Chailease
(5871 TT), Wan Hai (2615 TT), an E-Ink (8069 TT) will be removed. Kinsus (3189
TT) did not get added due to its disposition status. See below and this report for
our flows estimate.
Risk. Risk reward level here is not attractive, especially when the rebound came so
aggressively. Momentum to sustain the rally is not constructive, so investors should
be cautious of more volatility and further unwind (if there's anything to be

unwound). Jensen and Nvidia's headlines are certainly not helping, though I did see
some funny memes on the social media. Credit is another risk here; neoclouds top
line is +ve but let's not forget at what cost are these companies carving those
revenue from. Otherwise, market seems to process the macro prints well, with now
expecting a rate hike in Dec'26 and Mar'27. Last but not least, cross-strait tension
can be a focus next during Trump-Xi meeting at the APEC Summit next week. I

know the Taiwanese has been (probably will be) indifferent and just another BAU,
but anything assertively negative comment on Taiwan might fret the macro
traders, such as Xi's comment just now.
My View. Not a BEAR so far. I do not think the market is broken, but I
acknowledge the downside risk has risen again and enlarged as I respect the
technicals. That said, I am still excited about next week, even though we have fewer
fundamental catalysts now. In the US, Nvidia is set to report next week. A lighter

book might fare better in today's environment. To end on a fun personal note, GTA
VI will finally come out after 13 years of waiting. So if you can't find me on
Bloomberg, try looking for me in the Vice City.

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk

and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or

際降價影響，下調全年營收增速至約6%，兩家公司
同樣期待明年不利因素緩解，加上多款新產品陸續
推出，預期將緩步走出低谷。微創機器人手術於
3Q26全球手術量年增率為15%，預期4Q26全球手
術量年增率約14.5%，3Q26美國達文西手術成長放
緩至僅10%，公司表示主因基期影響，預期未來因
擴大使用計畫降價3~5%取得量的成長，門診手術
使用量增及新的適應症，將使美國市場呈現穩定或
改善。

若醫療補助刪減延後，將有利於醫院，若ACA補貼
部分恢復將有利於ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人

數止跌回升。兩黨均支持對藥物管理福利機構(PBM)
的監管，已有法案讓Part D藥價與PBM的報酬脫鉤，
回扣必須全數轉交給計畫方，2028年生效，現業者
多將Part D回扣與價差收入轉為固定費用應對，對
激進者業務分拆的主張則可能以聽證、調查形式出
現而非馬上立法。但在州層級，阿肯色州禁止PBM
擁有藥局的法律在州政府上訴中，CVS已決定1H28
分拆藥局成兩家獨立公司應對。就財報部分，UNH
3Q26財報仍維持業者利潤優先於規模與核保紀律，
合約重定價與刪除不賺錢合約，運用AI改善營運效
率、提升服務品質，在藥物福利管理主動讓利降低
政策風險。聯合醫療保險(UHC)因保費重定價與醫
療成本管控抵銷投保人數萎縮，Optum Health退
出虧損市場，聚焦高回報價值導向醫療運營紀律，
使此兩業務部門利潤率提升優於預期。商業保險醫
療成本趨勢依然高，但已趨於穩定，醫療優勢計畫
(MA)利潤率已接近2%-4%目標區間上限，公司自
評2027/2028年接近75%會員可望維持在4星以上
方案。醫療補助仍虧損，將透過州費率調整將緩步

走出低谷。4Q26醫療成本趨勢雖因季節性因素走升，
為今年獲利低點，但明年多項利潤率改善可期，維
持兩位數獲利成長。

## Document 558: 凱基投顧_2382 廣達_向子慧_20261111

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_2382 廣達_向子慧_20261111.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 電子 ‧ 台灣

廣達 
(2382.TW/2382 TT)

3Q26 獲利符合預期；2027 年營業利益複合成長力道強勁 
重要訊息 
廣達公布 3Q26 EPS 為 7.08 元，符合凱基預估及市場共識，但毛利率與營業
利益率不如預期。管理層看好 2027 年伺服器營收展望，並指引 AI 與通用型
伺服器營收將年增雙位數。 
評論及分析 
獲利率不佳，但 3Q26每股盈餘仍大致符合預期。3Q26 EPS 為 7.08 元，季
減 5%、年增 66%，大致符合凱基預估。毛利率 4.8% 及營業利益率 2.9% 
均不如凱基預估，因 AI 伺服器營收增加。3Q26 伺服器營收季增，但高均價 
AI 伺服器出貨量略為季減，而通用型伺服器出貨量季增。再加上筆電出貨量
季減 17% 至 960 萬台 (雖優於指引)，稼動率降低與組合不利導致 3Q26 毛
利率小幅季減，而研發支出為新 AI 伺服器專案增加而使營業費用上升，導
致營業利益率季減。在 39 億元匯兌利益的彌補下，3Q26 EPS 大致符合預期。
1Q-3Q26 EPS 為 20.03 元，年增 48%，營業利益年增 41%，且 3Q26 營業
利益年增率加速至 75%。 
伺服器營收將是 2027 年獲利的主要助力。管理層指引 4Q26 AI 伺服器出貨
量將因機種轉換而季減，但通用型伺服器出貨量將季增，筆電出貨量則將季
減雙位數。因此凱基預期營收將與前一季持平，低於先前預期。預期 4Q26 
毛利率將面臨壓力，但營業利益率可望與前一季持穩。不過在 VR200 AI 伺
服器於 1Q27 顯著出貨下營收有機會季增。2027 年公司預期伺服器整體營
收將年增雙位數，占比升至 85-90%，高於 2026 年的 80-85%。AI 伺服器
營收應將成長高雙位數 (可能年增 50-100%)，受惠於 VR 機櫃 ASP 提高，加
上 ASIC AI 伺服器營收成長。ASIC AI 伺服器占 2027 年整體 AI 伺服器營收
比重應超過 10%，高於 2026 年的 8-10%，亦是 2027 年營收與利潤率成
長的關鍵催化劑。然而，2027 年筆電出貨量將年減 5-10%，符合業界趨勢，
2026 年則年減 10-20%。因此，筆電營收占比將由 2026 年的 10-20% 縮
減至 2027 年的 10% 以下。隨著 AI 伺服器營收成長 (主要因 VR200 機櫃 
ASP 較 GB300 機櫃高出 80%)，預期其 2027 年毛利率與營業利益率將年減。
營運資金仍是公司的挑戰，管理層正與客戶協商委售(consign)條件，目前佔
比有限。考量更新指引，凱基下修 2026-27 年營收與利潤率，並將 EPS 調
降至 2026 年之 26.09 元、2027 年之 31.06 元，2027 年營業利益仍將年增 
32%。AI 需求仍強勁，廣達預計 2027 年資本支出將由 2026 年的 400 億元
增至 400-450 億元，主要用於美國與泰國廠的公用設施及測試設施需求。 
投資建議 
凱基將目標價由 500 元降至 435 元，以 2027 年預估 EPS 的 14 倍為基礎，
並維持「增加持股」評等，因 2027-28 年 AI 伺服器營收及 EPS 成長穩健。 
投資風險 
筆電需求疲弱、AI 伺服器獲利率偏低、零組件成本上揚及缺料。 
增加持股‧維持 
  收盤價 November 11 (NT$)  298.0

焦點內容  
1. 3Q26 獲利率不佳，但每股盈餘 7.08 元符
合凱基預估。 
2. 管理層預期 Vera Rubin AI 機櫃將於  1Q27 
出貨更為顯著，因此 4Q26 營收在過渡期
間可能與前一季持平。 
3. 2027 年通用型與 AI 伺服器營收將成長雙
位數，且 AI 伺服器營收年增逾 50%，而
筆電出貨量將年減 5-10%。2027 年伺服
器營收占比可能達 85-90%，但利潤率將
面臨壓力。

廣達成立於 1988 年，為全球最大 NB ODM 廠，2024 年出貨量 
4，590 萬台，全球市佔 24-25%。主要客戶為全球前六大品牌
NB廠如 Apple ( 美)、HP (美)、華碩與宏碁等。全球員工人數 3萬
名以上。未來數年雲端將為成長動能來源，目前公司拓展之業務
領域，包括伺服器、all-in-one (AIO)、穿戴裝置和居家 AI 產品。
2025年伺服器業務約佔 70%營收，筆電營收比重降至 20-25%，
電動車營收比重下滑至 0-5%。

## Document 592: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_6499 益安_陳宣甫_20261109

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Takeaway_6499 益安_陳宣甫_20261109.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點摘要

益安 
(6499 TT, NT$73, 增加持股)

重點摘要 
1. 益安主力產品 UroCross（BPH 暫時性植入物）於 10M26 在美國德
州完成首例商業化手術，台灣許可證亦於 11M26 初取得。美國每
療程保險給付約 8,000-1 萬美元，出廠價約 6,000 美元，定價與
UroLift 相近；前期由公司自行推廣並控制費用，營收預期緩步向
上、不會立即大幅跳升 
2. 授權方面，公司傾向以 UroCross 子公司股權交易方式整體出售，
參考同業成交價約 3-6 億美元，目前與數家固定對象深入洽談，對
方未要求須先達一定銷量。Duett 樞紐試驗需收案約 72 例、目前
收案 30 多例，FDA 限制單一醫院收案數使進度放慢，公司表示不
一定要收完案才授權 
3. CDMO 為「益安 2.0」的現金流主軸，2Q26 營收新台幣 1.07 億
元，3Q26 毛利率約 20%、接近損益兩平；合併現金約新台幣 8-9
億元、年費用約 6 億元，2027 年費用預期持續收斂。我們認為，
隨量產專案增加，CDMO 毛利率有望朝公司所稱成熟期 40-50%靠
攏，搭配研發費用收斂，可逐步縮小本業虧損 
結論 
我們認為 UroCross 已完成美國首例商業化手術，並具備明確給付碼與
接近 UroLift 的定價，商業化驗證已到位，有利授權談判；資產切分至
子公司後可採股權交易，結構較當年 Cross-Seal 的資產交易單純。產
品 6 個月後取出、不留金屬植入物，正對應永久植入物因移除需求而
銷售封頂的痛點，是吸引 BPH 領域大廠的核心差異化。近期驗證點為
美國醫院擴點速度與授權洽談進展，我們看好授權落地後可為益安挹
注資金，支撐 CDMO 整合與新專案布局

UroCross 商業化進度 
 美國：取證後啟動前期商業化，從取證到可入院手術一般約需半年，
10M26 初於德州醫院完成首例商業化手術；後續逐步擴展至更多
醫院與手術中心，讓更多醫師完成認證 
 台灣：8M26 邀請美國 KOL 來台分享，讓台灣醫師先熟悉產品；許
可證已於 11M26 初取得，後續將陸續展開商業化活動 
 製造與給付： 
 製造由集團 CDMO 事業的美國子公司 Medeologix 負責，已開始
備料並進行量產可製造性優化，未來量大時傾向移回台灣生產以
降低成本 
 已與 KOL 及美國泌尿科醫學會溝通醫院可採用的給付碼，公司
表示進度順利

### Page 2

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

 推廣策略： 
 前期美國與台灣皆由公司自行推廣，逐一陪同醫師操作數例，確
保病人選擇與執行方式符合原設計，不採一次全面鋪貨 
 推廣費用採控制方式，公司人力不大幅增加，美國搭配 1099 外
部約聘業務，目前未規劃組建百人銷售團隊 
 營收預期緩步向上、不會立即大幅跳升，但因單價相對好，不至
於要到 10 年後才達到市場期待的目標

定價與給付 
 美國：有明確的保險給付碼與價格，每療程保險給付約 8,000-1 萬
美元；UroCross 出廠價約為保險給付的 6 成、約 6,000 美元，讓醫
院保有獲利空間，初期實際售價依各醫院採購議價有高有低 
 與 UroLift 比較：UroLift 牌價每支 900-950 美元，每次需對稱植入
4-6 支，出給醫院約 4,500-5,000 美元，兩者定價相近 
 台灣：大部分醫材剛上市時健保不太會給付，預期先以自費方式上
市，納入健保可能需 3-5 年；台灣私人保險較多，與醫院、醫師仍
需溝通，定價尚未定案 
 療效：UroCross 植入 6 個月後取出，目前追蹤 3 年效果仍維持，資
料至少涵蓋 42 個月

授權規劃 
 交易形式： 
 最終目標為授權或出售，公司最希望整體 buyout，需靠多方競
爭達成；集團傾向出售子公司，參考同業成交價約 3-6 億美元 
 過往案例的其他形式包括 global licensing deal 、大廠先投資並
取得經銷權與 right of first re fusal，或投資加 option right 與部
分經銷權；Olympus 收購 Medi-Tate 即先投資取得 option right ，
之後執行全數收購 
 一直有大廠來談獨家經銷權，公司認為尚未到該階段，希望爭取
更好的條件 
 交易結構： 
 UroCross 已切分至子公司，可做股權交易，最為乾淨，後續款
項仍可另行設計；益安持股約 80%，其餘由當初貢獻構想的醫
師持有 
 當年 Cross-Seal 掛在益安本體下，只能以資產交易出售給
Terumo，之後還需以複雜的 service agreement 支援後續開發；
有了該次經驗，各產品皆切分至不同子公司

### Page 3

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

 洽談進度： 
 目前有數家固定洽談對象，包括已有 BPH 產品線及在泌尿科著
墨較多的公司，雙方已簽署相關程序、談得相當深入，惟公司無
法透露進度 
 對方正進行 DD、諮詢 KOL，可能要求觀摩手術，之後再上呈高
層決策，過程與當年洽談 Terumo 案類似；潛在對象未要求
UroCross 須先達一定銷量 
 公司未給授權時程預估，目標越快越好，前提是條件須為股東所
接受 
 授權區域：以全球為目標，但希望保留台灣及部分亞洲區域；國際
大廠推廣時台灣多排第 3-4 順位，上市好幾年後才用得到，且大中
華區不一定每家大廠都想要。除台灣與美國外，暫無其他國家取證
規劃

競品比較 
 UroLift：年銷售高峰約 3-4 億美元；為永久植入物，取出須做破壞
性較大的手術，上市數年後醫師常接到移除需求、裝置意願下降，
銷售因此封頂 
 ProVerum、Zenflow：皆為永久植入物，由未上市新創公司自行銷
售；Zenflow 前陣子獲 Cook Medical 投資並取得經銷權，部分金額
為借款，公司表示這也是會考慮的合作方式 
 iTind：需放置 7 天，病患反映疼痛明顯、須服用大量止痛藥，醫師
使用意願低 
 能量類手術：TURP、Rezum、Aquablation 皆為不可逆的組織挖除
或消融，可能傷及神經 
 UroCross 差異化：設計為 6 個月後取出，體內不留金屬植入物，後
續磁振造影或癌症篩檢不受遮擋；定位於藥物治療（頭暈等副作用）
與手術（恢復期長、不可逆）之間的治療缺口 
 自行銷售路線：公司以 PROCEPT 為例，其自行銷售並 IPO，市值約
11 億美元，但至今仍虧損，主因 Sales & Marketing 費用高且需投
放設備；UroCross 為純耗材，ROE 理論上較佳

Duett（胸主動脈修復） 
 樞紐試驗進度：取證需收案約 72 例，目前收案 30 多例，近幾個月
增加不多；FDA 限制每家醫院收案 5 例，須開設更多醫院，3Q26
新增數家醫院後收案才陸續增加。目前約 5-10 家醫院，將先收滿
一輪再視需要增加；病患多為急性送醫且須簽署同意書，收案較不
易，安全性方面尚無 SAE 
 試驗設計：single arm 、不需解盲，主要終點為 30 天中風、死亡等
重大不良事件，與歷史手縫資料比較；手術時間縮短未列入終點，

### Page 4

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

僅做紀錄。先前 20 例無重大不良事件，FDA 因此同意延伸為樞紐
試驗 
 產品定位： 
 主動脈剝離置換手術須停止循環，血流中斷最好不超過 30 分鐘；
通往腦部的 3 條血管手縫每條約 15 分鐘，使用 Duett 每條約 1-
2 分鐘，可降低中風與下肢血栓風險，且縫合處不滲血 
 美國每年手術量約 2-3 萬例，亞洲更多，大中華區發生率較歐美
高；產品具完整專利保護 
 授權與競爭： 
 已有一些授權討論，公司表示不一定要收完案才授權 
 最接近的競品為 Terumo 自行開發的 RapidLink，剛開始臨床，
Terumo 亦為潛在授權對象 
 CryoLife（現 Artivion）曾以約 2 億美元收購一款主動脈剝離產
品，惟其為置於升主動脈的網狀支架，與 Duett 處理的 3 條血管
接合並非同類問題；Duett 資產同樣放在子公司底下

CDMO 
 策略定位：「益安 2.0」以 CDMO 建立穩定現金流，CDMO 與創新
醫材為兩大主軸；除 Terumo 款項外，營收皆來自 CDMO，台灣與
美國皆有據點 
 營收：2Q26 營收新台幣 1.07 億元，不含 Terumo 款項與授權金 
 客戶結構：約 2/3 客戶已進入臨床試量產階段，另有固定量產客戶；
醫材一旦選定製造廠，更換有一定難度。即將放量的重要客戶皆為
美國的心血管介入產品創新公司，募資能力佳 
 毛利率與獲利：3Q26 毛利率約 20%，仍在成長階段，更多專案量
產後可望改善；目前接近損益兩平、小幅虧損。公司表示醫材
CDMO 成熟時毛利率至少 40-50%，大型 CDMO 如 Integer、
Nordson 約 30-40%至 70%

財務與資本規劃 
 現金與費用：合併帳上現金約新台幣 8-9 億元；年費用約 6 億元，
因先前臨床試驗而偏高。2027 年研發費用持平或略減、銷售費用
略增，整體持續收斂，惟尚無明確預估 
 股權結構：晟德原持股約 28-29%，釋出部分持股後目前約 20%出
頭，益安仍為其權益法投資公司，短期內不會改變 
 資金規劃：2025 年底辦理增資，價格 90 元，短期無資金需求；若
授權成功，資金可能用於 CDMO 上下游與平行整合，或以投資、
育成方式參與新專案

## Document 618: 史上最大IPO蓄势待发：Anthropic IPO据称获约六倍超额认购

Source PDF: all report pdf/史上最大IPO蓄势待发：Anthropic IPO据称获约六倍超额认购.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

此前报道称，认购需求主要来⾃机构投资者。据招股书， Anthropic 计划 IPO 与私募配售合
计募资最⾼ 904 亿美元，公司完全稀释估值约 1.4 万亿美元。
摘要：
Anthropic 的 IPO 投资者需求持续升温。
据彭博社 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇周四最新报道， Anthropic 的⾸次公开募股（ IPO ）已获得约六倍超额认购，
意味着投资者认购需求达到此次发⾏股票数量的约六倍。就在前⼀天，彭博社报道还称，这笔
IPO 已经获得数倍超额认购。最新消息显⽰，随着投资者路演推进，市场认购热情进⼀步升温。
这笔 IPO 有望成为史上规模最⼤的股票上市交易之⼀。据 Anthropic 本周⼆提交的新版招股书，
公司计划以每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元的价格发⾏股票，公开发⾏部分最⾼募资约 643.5 亿美元。此外，公
司还计划通过私募配售向⼤型科技企业募集 260 亿美元，两部分合计募资规模最⾼可达 904 亿美
元，超过此前 SpaceX 创纪录的 IPO 规模。
认购需求达到发⾏规模六倍，机构投资者成为主要推动⼒

据本周三的报道， Anthropic IPO 获得数倍超额认购。报道援引知情⼈⼠称，认购需求主要来⾃
⼤型投资者提交的⼤额意向订单，⽽⾮散⼾投资者推动的短期投机热潮。
仅⼀天之后，彭博社周四的报道进⼀步指出，认购需求已达到发⾏股票数量的约六倍，显⽰这
笔⼤型 IPO 在路演初期就获得了强劲的机构投资者需求。
这也为 Anthropic 后续发⾏定价提供了更⼤的操作空间。此前， Anthropic 公布的 IPO 发⾏价区间
为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元，但随着投资者需求持续⾛强，承销银⾏已经在考虑提⾼发⾏价。英国《⾦
融时报》此前报道称，银⾏⽅⾯正在考虑将 Anthropic 的 IPO 估值提⾼⾄ 1.5 万亿美元或更⾼。
不过，当前认购倍数仍属于发⾏过程中的需求指标，最终发⾏价格、发⾏规模及认购结果仍可
能发⽣变化。
最⾼募资 904 亿美元， Anthropic 剑指史上最⼤ IPO
Anthropic 此次 IPO 规模极为庞⼤，除了⾯向公开市场发⾏股票，公司还安排了⼤型科技企业参

与的私募配售。
根据提交给美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）的⽂件， Anthropic 计划公开发⾏约 8.25 亿股股票，发
⾏价区间为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元，按照区间上限计算，公开发⾏部分募资约 643.5 亿美元。
与此同时，公司还计划通过私募配售募集 260 亿美元，投资者包括 AMD 、博通、⾕歌、微软、英
伟达、迈威尔以及台积电等⼤型科技企业。
两部分融资合计最⾼达 904 亿美元。如果顺利完成，这笔交易的规模将超过 SpaceX 此前创纪录
的 IPO ，成为全球资本市场历史上规模最⼤的上市融资交易之⼀。
Anthropic 计划在纳斯达克全球精选市场上市，股票代码为 ANT 。⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利担任此次
IPO 的主承销商，另有 20 家银⾏参与承销。

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

平。随着近期 AI 科技股遭遇抛售，市场对 AI 企业巨额算⼒投⼊、未来盈利能⼒和投资回报的担忧
升温，估值预期也⼀度有所回落。
如今， IPO 获得约六倍超额认购，为 Anthropic 的发⾏前景提供了积极信号，也显⽰⼤型机构投
资者仍愿意为头部 AI 企业的⻓期增⻓前景投⼊巨额资⾦。
不过，强劲的发⾏认购需求并不意味着上市后股价必然上涨。 Anthropic 上市后的表现，还将取

决于投资者对其营收增⻓、盈利前景以及巨额 AI 基础设施投⼊回报的持续评估。
随着 Anthropic 推进上市，这笔超⼤规模 IPO 也将成为检验资本市场 AI 投资热情的重要试⾦⽯。

## Document 676: 日本と英国の米ドル建て名目GDPの逆転を考える

Source PDF: all report pdf/日本と英国の米ドル建て名目GDPの逆転を考える.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 11 月 6 日

MURC Focus

日本と英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の逆転を考える

～円安が経済成長につながっていないことが問題～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 日英の米ドル建て名目 GDP が 2027 年に逆転 
国際通貨基金（IMF）が今年 10 月に発表した最新の『世界経済見通し』の中で、2027 年にも日本と英国の米
ドル建て名目 GDP（国内総生産）が逆転するとの予測が示された（図表 1）。日本の米ドル建て名目 GDP はコロ
ナショック後の 2022 年に 5 兆ドルの節目を割り込んで以降、低迷が続いている。対照的に、英国の米ドル建て
名目 GDP はコロナショック以降に増加が顕著である。

所得水準の国際比較に用いられる一人当たりの米ドル建て名目 GDP（図表 2）も、コロナショック前までは日英
ともにほぼ 4 万ドル程度で拮抗していた。一方、コロナショック以降の英国は一人当たり GDP の増加が目覚まし
いが、反面で日本は低迷が続いている。こうしたトレンドが今後も続くと IMF は予想しているわけだが、その理由
は一体どこにあるのだろうか。 
図表 1. 米ドル建て名目 GDP 図表 2. 一人当たり米ドル建て名目 GDP 
（注）2026 年以降は推計値。

（注）2026 年以降は推計値。

〇IMF が今年10月に発表した最新の『世界経済見通し』の中で、2027年にも日本と英国の米ドル建て名目
GDP が逆転するとの予測が示された。 
〇一方、コロナショック以降の英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加は高インフレを主因とするものであり、英国
民の所得の増加を反映したものとは言い難い。 
〇経済成長を伴わない英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加も問題だが、一方で日本の米ドル建て名目 GDP
の減少も大きな問題である。日本も、円安に依存するこれまでの考え方を冷静に見つめ直すべきだろう。

### Page 2

2. 物価要因と為替要因に起因する英国の GDP 急増 
2020 年以降の英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の推移を①成長要因（実質 GDP の成長率）と②為替要因（対ド
ルレートの変化率）、③物価要因（GDP デフレーターの変化率）に分解してみたものが図表 3 である。これによる
と、特に 2023 年以降の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加は主に物価要因（高インフレ）で説明でき、それを為替要因
（ポンド高）が増幅している関係にある。

言い換えると、コロナショック以降の英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加は、成長要因（好景気）に伴う現象と
は評価できない。加えて 2025 年以降は、ポンドの実質ベースでの対米ドルレートが名目ベースを上回っており、
ポンドが割高に評価されていると判断される（図表 4）。このポンドの過大評価は、この間に相場が急騰したユーロ
と同様に、米トランプ政権発の“ドル不安”の動きによるところが大きいと考えられる。

IMF は 2027 年以降も英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP は増加が続くと予測しているが、その主因は引き続き物
価要因（高インフレ）であり、次いで為替要因（ポンド高）となる。しかしポンドが米国発の流れの中で過大評価さ
れている可能性に鑑みれば、米トランプ政権のレームダック化などでドル不安が和らいだり、あるいは英国を含む
欧州発のショックでポンド安が生じたりすれば、一転して急速なポンド安となる恐れがある。

いずれにしても、コロナショック以降の英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加は高インフレを主因とするものであ
り、英国民の所得の増加を反映したものとは言い難い。ポンド高に関しても、いわゆるドル不安の中で過大評価
が進んだ可能性があるため、何らかのショックで急速なポンド安に転じる懸念もある。このシナリオが現実のものと
なった場合、英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP は急減することになるだろう。

図表 3. 英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の推移 図表 4. ポンドの対米ドルレート指数

### Page 3

3. 円安が経済成長につながらなかった日本 
対照的に、コロナショック以降の日本の米ドル建て名目 GDP は減少が続いているが、それは主に為替要因
（円安）によるものである（図表 5）。2023 年以降は日本経済がインフレ基調に転じ、物価要因が押し上げ寄与と
なっているが、一方で成長要因は小幅なプラスにとどまっている。円安は日本経済にとってプラスとの主張もある
が、実際は円安が経済成長に必ずしもつながっていないことが読み取れる。

他方で、円の対米ドルレート指数（図表 6）は、2021 年頃から実質レートが名目レートを上回るピッチで下落し
ている。日米間の物価上昇の差が著しいためだが、このことは、円が過小評価されている可能性が高いことを意
味している。一方、歴代の日本の政権は、円安による景気浮揚効果を重視してきたが、それが経済成長の加速
につながらなかったことは明らかである。

円相場がわずかに持ち直した 2025 年に成長要因が押し上げ寄与に転じているように、現代の日本の経済構
造は、円安であれば輸出が伸びて成長が加速するといった 1990 年代までの構造とは異なる。一方、今年 2 月に
生じたイラン発のエネルギーショックによってインフレ圧力が高まる中でも、円安の是正が進まないばかりか一段
の円安となったことでモノの輸入コストが膨らみ、日本からさらなる所得流出が生じている。

経済成長を伴わない英国の米ドル建て名目 GDP の増加も問題だが、一方で日本の米ドル建て名目 GDP の
減少も大きな問題である。健全な経済成長の下で米ドル建て名目 GDP を増加させていくためには、英国も日本
もまた生産性の向上に代表される経済の体質改善を進める必要がある。特に日本の場合、歴代の政権が円の実
質的な切り下げに活路を見出してきたが、それが経済成長の加速につながらなかった。

今回の日英の名目 GDP の逆転をきっかけに、日本は円と経済成長のあり方に対して、これまでの考え方を冷
静に見つめ直すべきではないだろうか。 
図表 5. 日本の米ドル建て名目 GDP の推移 図表 6. 円の対米ドルレート指数

### Page 1

⻩仁勋辞职余震未消，下周 AI 赛道要迎接 “ 双重⼤考 ” ：英伟达 “ 后⻩时代 ” ⾸份财报， " 如果
业绩低于预期，市场给予的惩罚⼒度将会被放⼤ " ； Anthropic 则携万亿估值重磅 IPO ，两⼤
悬念接连叩关，将直接决定全球资本的 AI 信仰能否延续。
摘要：
英伟达 CEO 离职⻛波尚未平息，华尔街已将⽬光投向下周 —— 英伟达季报与 Anthropic 上市将接
连登场， AI 交易的信⼼能否经受住这轮双重压⼒测试，答案即将揭晓。
英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋因婚外情丑闻于上周四突然辞职，引发市场对这家 AI 芯⽚巨头的深度担忧。
尽管如此，英伟达股价本周跌幅约 13% ，远⼩于此前市场担忧的 20% 乃⾄更⼤跌幅，标普 500 指
数更录得 8 ⽉以来最佳单周表现。与此同时， Anthropic 宣布其 IPO 估值较此前约 2 万亿美元的⾼
点⼤幅下调，预计本次上市估值约为 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.4 万亿美元。
市场所受的冲击，折射出当前 AI 叙事的脆弱性。消费者通胀数据⾼于预期、美联储进⼀步加息预

期升温，叠加中东局势持续紧张，构成不可忽视的宏观压⼒。
Wells Fargo Investment Institute 全球股票和实物资产主管 Sameer Samana 警告称，若下周两
⼤事件均令⼈失望， " 可能最终让投资者从这种 ' 凡事往好处看 ' 的状态中警醒过来 " 。
英伟达：财报将验证后⻩仁勋时代的稳定性
英伟达将于周⼆美股收盘后公布财季业绩，这是⻩仁勋离任后的⾸份财报，外界关注度极⾼。
据路透援引 LSEG 数据，分析师预计英伟达第三财季营收约为 1090 亿美元，盈利同⽐增⻓约 90%
—— 市场对业绩本⾝的预期较为乐观，但投资者更关注的是公司管理层的稳定性与战略延续
性。
继任者问题成为焦点。英伟达⾸席财务官、临时 CEO Colette Kress 将承压出席业绩发布，外
界期待她为投资者吃下 " 定⼼丸 " 。
这位在英伟达任职 13 年、此前供职于思科和微软的⾼管，或许不具备⻩仁勋式的个⼈魅⼒，但
分析⼈⼠认为她能为公司提供稳健过渡所需的管理连续性。据报道，英伟达超⼤规模计算与⾼

性能计算副总裁 Ian Buck ⽬前是正式继任者的早期热⻔⼈选。
此外， Colette Kress 还需就⻩仁勋任内签订的⼀系列融资交易向投资者作出说明，这些交易⽬
前仍存在诸多不确定性。
F/m Investments ⾸席执⾏官 Alex Morris 指出，考虑到围绕英伟达的不确定性， " 如果业绩低于
预期，市场给予的惩罚⼒度将会被放⼤ " 。
然⽽，部分投资者已将当前股价视为买⼊机会。英伟达股价六周内累计下跌逾 30% ，市值从 10
⽉初约 5.8 万亿美元缩⽔⾄约 3.9 万亿美元。
KKM Financial 创始⼈ Jeff Kilburg 表⽰本周已买⼊ 1 ⽉份⾏权价 200 美元的看涨期权，彼时股价约
为 160 美元。 " 他们经过验证的需求达到⼀万亿美元，从 Blackwell 到 Ruben ，可⻅度延伸⾄ 2028
年，这不会消失， "Kilburg 说。 Yorkville Ives & Co 合伙⼈ Dan Ives 则维持英伟达 12 个⽉⽬标价

225 美元，将现价视为买⼊机会。

Anthropic ：万亿估值 IPO 叩关， AI 情绪晴⾬表
就在英伟达财报发布两天后， Claude 聊天机器⼈开发商 Anthropic 预计将于周四登陆纳斯达克全
球精选市场，股票代码为 "ANT" ， IPO 定价区间为每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元，计划发售 8.25 亿股，募资规

元。

Anthropic 预计 2026 年底将实现的 1000 亿美元年化营收计算，当前定价约对应 12 倍 ARR （年度

经常性收⼊）， " 甚⾄算不上很贵 " 。

⽰，希望公司能在 IPO 上 " 少承诺、多兑现 " 。 Black 则指出，他从未⻅过哪家公司在⼀级市场受到
如此⾼的投资者追捧， "Anthropic 聪明地让出了⼀些价格，让公众也有钱可赚，我认为这个定价
会表现得相当不错。 "
此次 IPO 紧随埃隆 · ⻢斯克旗下 SpaceX 于今年 6 ⽉成功登陆市场之后 ——SpaceX 以近 1.75 万亿美
元估值募资约 750 亿美元，不过该股上市后表现起伏，从 IPO 价格 135 美元⼀度跌⾄ 8 ⽉的约 105
美元，最新报约 139 美元。
Madison Investments ⾸席投资策略师 Patrick Ryan 警告称，若 Anthropic 上市后表现不佳， " 这
可能是另⼀个警⽰信号，表明投资者对 AI 交易开始感到更多压⼒ " 。

黃仁勳離任：四種技術升級情境

01 接班順利 既定技術路線延續 全球升級大致維持；台廠影響有限
02 接班磨合 部分決策、量產延後 部分領域升級放慢；2027 年獲利遞延
03 技術領導缺口 產品整合與架構決策受阻 系統級升級可能放慢；高階供應鏈 EPS 承壓
04 競爭者快速補位 NVIDIA 放慢；ASIC、AMD 加速 整體創新未必放慢；台廠出現明顯分化
觀察重點：2027 年既定產品不會自動停擺；更需追蹤 2028 年後的新架構決策、供應鏈協調與商業化速度。

## Document 810: 黄仁勋再次被曝与第二名女员工有染

Source PDF: all report pdf/黄仁勋再次被曝与第二名女员工有染.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
据媒体报道，已被罢免的英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋曾与第⼆名员⼯有过亲密关系。此前⻩仁勋被
曝有两段婚外情，分别是 Alice Huang 以及并英伟达医疗保健业务副总裁 Kimberly
Powell 。
摘要：
据路透社从两位知情⼈⼠处获悉，已被罢免的英伟达（ Nvidia ）⾸席执⾏官⻩仁勋（ Jensen
Huang ）曾与第⼆名员⼯有过亲密关系。
在此消息曝光之前，⻩仁勋还曾曝出另外两段婚外情，并因此于周四辞职。
这两名⼥性分别是爱丽丝 · ⻩（ Alice Huang ），以及英伟达医疗保健业务副总裁⾦伯利 · 鲍威尔
（ Kimberly Powell ）。
媒体未公布这名（第⼆位）员⼯的姓名，且未能成功联系到她。
在有关他与爱丽丝 · ⻩关系的报道传出后，⻩仁勋曾在 11 ⽉ 8 ⽇的⼀份声明中承认有过婚外情，但

未直接点名提及她。
截⾄发稿，周四盘中英伟达股价下跌 6% 。
